```python
"""
Searches nonnegative step-function heights minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a, a)) / (sum(a))^2
It combines:
  - analytic low-autocorrelation "power-tail" seeds,
  - smooth-max projected gradient descent,
  - exact-ish coordinate line searches,
  - 1D mass-transfer line searches,
  - occasional linear-programming directions.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
    SCIPY_OPT_OK = True
except Exception:
    SCIPY_OPT_OK = False


def fast_convolve(a):
    """Full linear convolution a*a via FFT (or direct for tiny n)."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    if n <= 96:
        return np.convolve(a, a)
    size = 2 * n - 1
    fft_size = 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, fft_size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, fft_size)[:size]
    return c


def conv_filter(a, b):
    """Full linear convolution a*b via FFT."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    na, nb = a.size, b.size
    if na == 0 or nb == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    b = np.where(np.isfinite(b), b, 0.0)
    size = na + nb - 1
    fft_size = 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, fft_size)
    B = np.fft.rfft(b, fft_size)
    return np.fft.irfft(A * B, fft_size)[:size]


def normalize_seq(a):
    """Clip to nonnegative finite values and normalize to sum 1."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / a.size
    return a / s


def project_simplex(v):
    """Euclidean projection onto the probability simplex."""
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return v
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    cond = u * np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64) > (cssv - 1.0)
    if not cond.any():
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    rho = int(np.nonzero(cond)[0][-1])
    theta = (cssv[rho] - 1.0) / (rho + 1.0)
    out = v - theta
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    return out / s


def quick_score(a):
    """Fast internal estimate of the objective using FFT convolution."""
    a = normalize_seq(a)
    if a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    c = fast_convolve(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * a.size * np.max(c))


def try_candidate(a, best_a, best_score, label=""):
    """Use evaluate_sequence to accept a candidate if it is better."""
    if a is None:
        return best_a, best_score
    a = normalize_seq(a)
    if a.size == 0:
        return best_a, best_score
    try:
        score = float(evaluate_sequence(a.tolist()))
    except Exception:
        score = float("inf")
    if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score - 1e-12:
        print(f"NEW BEST {label}: {score:.6f} n={a.size}")
        return a, score
    return best_a, best_score


def add_seed_info(infos, label, a):
    """Append (label, normalized_a, quick_score) to seed_infos."""
    a = normalize_seq(a)
    if a.size == 0:
        return
    qs = quick_score(a)
    if np.isfinite(qs):
        infos.append((label, a, float(qs)))


def seed_score_key(item):
    return item[2]


def make_alpha_tail_seed(n, alpha=0.5):
    """
    Coefficients of (1-x)^(-alpha) for x near 1, i.e.
    a[k] proportional to product_{i=0}^{k-1} (i+alpha)/(i+1).
    alpha=1 gives uniform; alpha=0.5 gives central-binomial-style decay.
    """
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.isfinite(alpha):
        alpha = 0.5
    if alpha <= 1e-8:
        alpha = 0.05
    if alpha >= 1.0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[0] = 1.0
    prev = 1.0
    for i in range(1, n):
        prev = prev * ((i - 1) + alpha) / float(i)
        a[i] = prev
    return normalize_seq(np.maximum(a, 0.0))


def make_tilted_tail_seed(n, alpha=0.5, tilt=0.0):
    """Tail seed with a linear weighting to tune shape."""
    a = make_alpha_tail_seed(n, alpha)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 1:
        return a
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    w = 1.0 + float(tilt) * x
    w = np.maximum(w, 1e-12)
    return normalize_seq(a * w)


def make_exponential_seed(n, theta=1.0):
    """Exponential one-sided seed."""
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([1.0])
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.exp(-float(theta) * i / max(1, n - 1))
    return normalize_seq(a)


def make_sine_seed(n, mode=1, alpha=0.8, phase=0.0):
    """Anti-symmetric sinusoidal seed: (1 + alpha*u)/n."""
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    u = np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * float(mode) * i / float(n) + float(phase))
    amax = float(np.max(np.abs(u)))
    if amax <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    u /= amax
    a = (1.0 + float(alpha) * u) / float(n)
    return normalize_seq(a)


def make_random_antisy_seed(n, num_modes=8, alpha=0.8, seed=123):
    """Random low-order sine mixture, normalized to max absolute 1."""
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    u = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for m in range(1, int(num_modes) + 1):
        coeff = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0 / np.sqrt(float(m)))
        u += coeff * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * float(m) * i / float(n))
    amax = float(np.max(np.abs(u)))
    if amax <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    u /= amax
    a = (1.0 + float(alpha) * u) / float(n)
    return normalize_seq(a)


def project_complement(v):
    """
    Project to the strong anti-palindromic face
    a[i] + a[n-1-i] = 2/n (middle fixed for odd n).
    """
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return v
    v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    half = n // 2
    for i in range(half):
        j = n - 1 - i
        s = float(n) * (v[i] - v[j])
        if s > 1.0:
            s = 1.0
        elif s < -1.0:
            s = -1.0
        a[i] = (1.0 + s) / float(n)
        a[j] = (1.0 - s) / float(n)
    if n % 2 == 1:
        a[half] = 1.0 / float(n)
    return normalize_seq(a)


def interpolate_to_length(a, new_n):
    """Linearly resample a sequence to a new length."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    old_n = a.size
    new_n = int(new_n)
    if new_n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if old_n == 0:
        return np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64) / new_n
    if old_n == new_n:
        return normalize_seq(a)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old_n)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n)
    out = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    return normalize_seq(out)


def smooth_gradient_from_conv(a, conv, beta):
    """
    Gradient of the smooth maximum logsumexp(beta*conv)/beta,
    projected to the tangent space sum(d)=0.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    conv = np.asarray(conv, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    L = 2 * n - 1
    if conv.size < L:
        tmp = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        if conv.size > 0:
            tmp[:conv.size] = conv[:L]
        conv = tmp
    else:
        conv = conv[:L]

    if beta <= 1e-6:
        w = np.full(L, 1.0 / float(L), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        maxb = float(np.max(conv))
        ex = np.exp(float(beta) * (conv - maxb))
        s = float(ex.sum())
        if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
            w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
            w[int(np.argmax(conv))] = 1.0
        else:
            w = ex / s

    c = conv_filter(a, w[::-1])
    need = n - 1
    if c.size < 2 * n - 1:
        tmp = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
        if c.size > 0:
            tmp[:c.size] = c
        c = tmp
    grad = 2.0 * c[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]
    grad = grad - float(np.mean(grad))
    return grad


def smooth_descent(a0, time_limit, lr=0.005, mom=0.88,
                   beta_start=2.0, beta_end=2000.0, seed=123, verbose=False):
    """Projected gradient on smooth max, with momentum and annealing beta."""
    a = normalize_seq(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a, float("inf")
    conv = fast_convolve(a)
    best_obj = float(np.max(conv))
    best_a = a.copy()
    prev_obj = best_obj
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    beta = float(beta_start)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    it = 0
    no_improve = 0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit:
        it += 1
        grad = smooth_gradient_from_conv(a, conv, beta)
        v = mom * v - lr * grad
        a_new = project_simplex(a + v)
        conv_new = fast_convolve(a_new)
        if conv_new.size == 0:
            break
        obj_new = float(np.max(conv_new))
        if not np.isfinite(obj_new):
            a = best_a.copy()
            conv = fast_convolve(a)
            v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            no_improve = 0
            continue

        if obj_new < best_obj - 1e-15:
            best_obj = obj_new
            best_a = a_new.copy()
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if obj_new > prev_obj + 1e-14:
            v = 0.5 * v
        prev_obj = obj_new
        a = a_new
        conv = conv_new

        beta *= 1.02
        if beta > float(beta_end):
            beta = float(beta_end)

        if no_improve > 260:
            scale = 0.003 + 0.002 * min(3, no_improve // 260)
            a = project_simplex(best_a + rng.standard_normal(n) * scale)
            conv = fast_convolve(a)
            prev_obj = float(np.max(conv))
            v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            no_improve = 0

        if verbose and it % 2000 == 0:
            print(f"    smooth iter {it}: best_obj={best_obj:.12f}, beta={beta:.2f}")

    a = normalize_seq(best_a)
    conv = fast_convolve(a)
    obj = float(np.max(conv))
    return a, obj


def coordinate_descent_pass(a0, max_time=30.0, grid_points=14,
                            top_k=8, max_candidates=28, recompute=True):
    """
    Coordinate descent. For each coordinate j, solve the exact 1D subproblem
    a[j] = x, others scaled to keep sum=1, and choose x from a robust set of
    candidate points (grid + active-constraint intersections).
    """
    a = normalize_seq(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a, float("inf")
    L = 2 * n - 1
    conv = fast_convolve(a)
    current_obj = float(np.max(conv))
    best_obj = current_obj
    best_a = a.copy()
    t0 = time.monotonic()

    if n > 1200:
        grid_points = min(int(grid_points), 8)
        top_k = min(int(top_k), 4)
        max_candidates = min(int(max_candidates), 12)

    update_count = 0

    for j in range(n):
        if time.monotonic() - t0 > max_time:
            break

        xold = float(a[j])
        s = 1.0 - xold
        if s < 1e-8:
            continue

        b = a.copy()
        b[j] = 0.0
        b = b / s

        D = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        st = j
        en = min(j + n, L)
        if en > st:
            D[st:en] = b[:en - st]

        E = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        ej = 2 * j
        if ej < L:
            E[ej] = 1.0

        # a = x e_j + s b, so conv(a) = x^2 E + 2 x s D + s^2 B
        B = (conv - xold * xold * E - 2.0 * xold * s * D) / (s * s)
        B = np.maximum(B, 0.0)

        A = E - 2.0 * B
        Bc = 2.0 * (D - B)
        Cc = B

        xs_list = []
        if grid_points > 0:
            xs_list.extend(np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(grid_points)).tolist())
        xs_list.extend([xold, 0.0, 1.0])

        if top_k > 0 and L > 0:
            K = min(int(top_k), L)
            if K == L:
                top = np.arange(L)
            else:
                top = np.argpartition(conv, -K)[-K:]

            for p in top:
                p = int(p)
                if abs(A[p]) > 1e-15:
                    r = -Bc[p] / (2.0 * A[p])
                    if -1e-12 <= r <= 1.0 + 1e-12:
                        xs_list.append(float(np.clip(r, 0.0, 1.0)))

            m = len(top)
            for ii in range(m):
                p = int(top[ii])
                for jj in range(ii + 1, m):
                    q = int(top[jj])
                    da = A[p] - A[q]
                    db = Bc[p] - Bc[q]
                    dc = Cc[p] - Cc[q]
                    if abs(da) > 1e-14:
                        disc = db * db - 4.0 * da * dc
                        if disc >= -1e-14:
                            if disc < 0.0:
                                disc = 0.0
                            sq = np.sqrt(disc)
                            r1 = (-db - sq) / (2.0 * da)
                            r2 = (-db + sq) / (2.0 * da)
                            for r in (r1, r2):
                                if -1e-12 <= r <= 1.0 + 1e-12 and len(xs_list) < 220:
                                    xs_list.append(float(np.clip(r, 0.0, 1.0)))
                    elif abs(db) > 1e-14:
                        r = -dc / db
                        if -1e-12 <= r <= 1.0 + 1e-12 and len(xs_list) < 220:
                            xs_list.append(float(np.clip(r, 0.0, 1.0)))

        xs = np.asarray(xs_list, dtype=np.float64)
        xs = xs[(xs >= 0.0) & (xs <= 1.0)]
        if xs.size == 0:
            continue
        xs = np.sort(xs)

        uniq = []
        last = None
        for val in xs:
            val = float(val)
            if last is None or val - last > 1e-8:
                uniq.append(val)
                last = val
        xs = np.asarray(uniq, dtype=np.float64)

        if xs.size > max_candidates:
            idx = np.linspace(0, xs.size - 1, int(max_candidates)).astype(int)
            xs = xs[idx]
            xs = np.concatenate((xs, np.array([0.0, 1.0, float(xold)], dtype=np.float64)))
            xs = np.sort(xs)
            uniq = []
            last = None
            for val in xs:
                val = float(val)
                if last is None or val - last > 1e-8:
                    uniq.append(val)
                    last = val
            xs = np.asarray(uniq, dtype=np.float64)
            if xs.size > max_candidates:
                xs = xs[:max_candidates]

        best_val = current_obj
        best_x = xold
        chunk_size = max(1, 200000 // max(1, L))
        for start in range(0, xs.size, chunk_size):
            chunk = xs[start:start + chunk_size]
            ox = 1.0 - chunk
            vals = (ox * ox)[:, None] * B + (2.0 * chunk * ox)[:, None] * D + (chunk * chunk)[:, None] * E
            mvals = vals.max(axis=1)
            idx_local = int(np.argmin(mvals))
            if mvals[idx_local] < best_val:
                best_val = float(mvals[idx_local])
                best_x = float(chunk[idx_local])

        if best_val < current_obj - 1e-13 and 0.0 < best_x < 1.0:
            refine = np.linspace(max(0.0, best_x - 0.04), min(1.0, best_x + 0.04), 16)
            refine = np.concatenate((refine, np.array([best_x], dtype=np.float64)))
            ox = 1.0 - refine
            vals = (ox * ox)[:, None] * B + (2.0 * refine * ox)[:, None] * D + (refine * refine)[:, None] * E
            mvals = vals.max(axis=1)
            idx_ref = int(np.argmin(mvals))
            if mvals[idx_ref] < best_val:
                best_val = float(mvals[idx_ref])
                best_x = float(refine[idx_ref])

        if best_val < current_obj - 1e-14 and abs(best_x - xold) > 1e-12:
            a_new = a.copy()
            if best_x <= 1e-15:
                a_new[j] = 0.0
            elif best_x >= 1.0 - 1e-15:
                a_new[:] = 0.0
                a_new[j] = 1.0
            else:
                a_new[j] = best_x
                mask = np.ones(n, dtype=bool)
                mask[j] = False
                a_new[mask] *= (1.0 - best_x) / s
                a_new = np.maximum(a_new, 0.0)
                ss = float(a_new.sum())
                if ss > 1e-15:
                    a_new = a_new / ss

            conv_new = ((1.0 - best_x) * (1.0 - best_x)) * B \
                       + (2.0 * best_x * (1.0 - best_x)) * D \
                       + (best_x * best_x) * E
            conv_new = np.maximum(conv_new, 0.0)
            new_obj = float(np.max(conv_new))

            if new_obj < best_obj - 1e-15:
                best_obj = new_obj
                best_a = a_new.copy()

            a = a_new
            conv = conv_new
            current_obj = new_obj
            update_count += 1

            if update_count % 32 == 0:
                a = normalize_seq(a)
                conv = fast_convolve(a)
                current_obj = float(np.max(conv))
                if current_obj < best_obj - 1e-15:
                    best_obj = current_obj
                    best_a = a.copy()

    a = normalize_seq(best_a)
    conv = fast_convolve(a)
    obj = float(np.max(conv))
    return a, obj


def suggested_pairs(a, conv, max_pairs=80):
    """Generate promising (increase_i, decrease_j) transfer pairs."""
    n = a.size
    L = conv.size
    if n <= 1 or L <= 0:
        return []
    K = min(30, L)
    top = np.argpartition(conv, -K)[-K:]
    seen = set()
    pairs = []

    for k in top:
        k = int(k)
        s = max(0, k - n + 1)
        e = min(n - 1, k)
        if e < s:
            continue
        idx = np.arange(s, e + 1)
        vals = a[idx]
        ploc = int(np.argmin(vals))
        qloc = int(np.argmax(vals))
        p = int(idx[ploc])
        q = int(idx[qloc])
        if p != q:
            key = (p, q)
            if key not in seen:
                seen.add(key)
                pairs.append(key)
                if len(pairs) >= max_pairs:
                    break

    if len(pairs) < max_pairs:
        order = np.argsort(a)
        lows = order[:max(1, n // 10)]
        highs = order[::-1][:max(1, n // 10)]
        for q in highs:
            for p in lows:
                p = int(p)
                q = int(q)
                if p != q:
                    key = (p, q)
                    if key not in seen:
                        seen.add(key)
                        pairs.append(key)
                        if len(pairs) >= max_pairs:
                            break
            if len(pairs) >= max_pairs:
                break

    return pairs


def transfer_polish(a0, max_time=30.0, grid_points=18, rng_seed=123, verbose=False):
    """
    1D line searches along mass-transfer directions a + t(e_i - e_j),
    which preserves the sum exactly.
    """
    a = normalize_seq(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 1:
        conv = fast_convolve(a)
        return a, float(np.max(conv))
    L = 2 * n - 1
    conv = fast_convolve(a)
    current_obj = float(np.max(conv))
    best_obj = current_obj
    best_a = a.copy()
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(rng_seed))

    pairs = suggested_pairs(a, conv, 100)
    pidx = 0
    no_improve = 0
    trial = 0

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < max_time:
        trial += 1
        if pidx < len(pairs):
            i, j = pairs[pidx]
            pidx += 1
        else:
            if trial % 800 == 0:
                pairs = suggested_pairs(a, conv, 60)
                pidx = 0
            i = int(rng.integers(0, n))
            j = int(rng.integers(0, n))
            if i == j:
                continue

        ai = float(a[i])
        aj = float(a[j])
        if ai + aj < 1e-15:
            continue

        C = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        C[i:i + n] = a
        C[j:j + n] -= a

        E = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        if 2 * i < L:
            E[2 * i] += 1.0
        ij = i + j
        if ij < L:
            E[ij] -= 2.0
        if 2 * j < L:
            E[2 * j] += 1.0

        lo = -ai
        hi = aj
        if hi <= lo + 1e-15:
            continue

        xs = np.linspace(lo, hi, int(grid_points))
        best_x = 0.0
        best_val = current_obj
        chunk_size = max(1, 200000 // max(1, L))
        for start in range(0, xs.size, chunk_size):
            chunk = xs[start:start + chunk_size]
            vals = conv + (2.0 * chunk)[:, None] * C + (chunk * chunk)[:, None] * E
            mvals = vals.max(axis=1)
            idx_local = int(np.argmin(mvals))
            if mvals[idx_local] < best_val:
                best_val = float(mvals[idx_local])
                best_x = float(chunk[idx_local])

        if best_val < current_obj - 1e-13 and abs(best_x) > 1e-15:
            a_new = a.copy()
            a_new[i] += best_x
            a_new[j] -= best_x
            a_new = np.maximum(a_new, 0.0)
            ss = float(a_new.sum())
            if ss > 1e-15:
                a_new = a_new / ss

            conv_new = conv + 2.0 * best_x * C + best_x * best_x * E
            conv_new = np.maximum(conv_new, 0.0)
            new_obj = float(np.max(conv_new))

            if new_obj < current_obj - 1e-14:
                a = a_new
                conv = fast_convolve(a)
                current_obj = float(np.max(conv))
                if current_obj < best_obj - 1e-15:
                    best_obj = current_obj
                    best_a = a.copy()
                no_improve = 0
            else:
                no_improve += 1
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if no_improve > 3500:
            a = project_simplex(best_a + rng.standard_normal(n) * 0.002)
            conv = fast_convolve(a)
            current_obj = float(np.max(conv))
            pairs = suggested_pairs(a, conv, 60)
            pidx = 0
            no_improve = 0

        if verbose and trial % 2000 == 0:
            print(f"    transfer trial {trial}: best_obj={best_obj:.12f}")

    a = normalize_seq(best_a)
    conv = fast_convolve(a)
    obj = float(np.max(conv))
    return a, obj


def lp_direction(f, M):
    """
    Solve the LP direction:
      maximize sum g
      subject to (f*g)_k <= M, g >= 0.
    """
    if not SCIPY_OPT_OK:
        return None
    n = f.size
    if n > 850:
        return None
    L = 2 * n - 1
    k = np.arange(L, dtype=np.int64)[:, None]
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)[None, :]
    idx = k - i
    mask = (idx >= 0) & (idx < n)
    A = np.zeros((L, n), dtype=np.float64)
    A[mask] = f[idx[mask]]
    c = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.full(L, float(M), dtype=np.float64)
    try:
        res = linprog(
            c,
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=b,
            bounds=(0.0, None),
            method="highs",
            options={"presolve": True}
        )
    except Exception:
        return None
    if res.success and res.x is not None:
        x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
        if np.isfinite(x).all() and x.sum() > 1e-12:
            return np.maximum(x, 0.0)
    return None


def lp_polish(a0, max_time=60.0, seed=123, max_solves=3):
    """Use LP best-response directions a <- (1-t)a + t g."""
    a = normalize_seq(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 1 or not SCIPY_OPT_OK or n > 850:
        conv = fast_convolve(a)
        return a, float(np.max(conv))

    conv = fast_convolve(a)
    best_obj = float(np.max(conv))
    best_a = a.copy()
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    solves = 0
    stall = 0

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < max_time and solves < int(max_solves):
        rem = max_time - (time.monotonic() - t0)
        if rem < 25.0:
            break
        conv = fast_convolve(a)
        M = float(np.max(conv))
        if M <= 1e-15:
            break
        g = lp_direction(a, M)
        solves += 1
        if g is None:
            break
        gs = float(g.sum())
        if gs <= 1e-12:
            break
        g = g / gs

        improved = False
        for t in (0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.3, 0.5, 0.7, 0.9):
            h = (1.0 - t) * a + t * g
            h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)
            hs = float(h.sum())
            if hs <= 1e-12:
                continue
            h = h / hs
            obj = float(np.max(fast_convolve(h)))
            if obj < best_obj - 1e-14:
                best_obj = obj
                best_a = h.copy()
                a = h
                improved = True
                break

        if improved:
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall >= 2:
                a = project_simplex(best_a + rng.standard_normal(n) * 0.003)
                stall = 0

    a = normalize_seq(best_a)
    conv = fast_convolve(a)
    obj = float(np.max(conv))
    return a, obj


def combined_polish(a0, time_limit, verbose=False, seed=123):
    """
    Fixed-length local optimizer combining several phase types.
    Returns (a, max_conv) where a is normalized sum 1.
    """
    a = normalize_seq(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a, float("inf")
    conv = fast_convolve(a)
    best_obj = float(np.max(conv))
    best_a = a.copy()
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    phase = 0
    no_improve = 0
    use_coord = n <= 1100
    use_lp = SCIPY_OPT_OK and (n <= 800)

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit:
        rem = time_limit - (time.monotonic() - t0)
        if rem < 8.0:
            break
        p = phase % 8

        if p == 0:
            alloc = min(18.0, rem)
            a_tmp, obj = smooth_descent(best_a, alloc, lr=0.006, mom=0.88,
                                        beta_start=2.0, beta_end=200.0,
                                        seed=seed + phase)
        elif p == 1:
            if use_coord:
                alloc = min(55.0, rem)
                a_tmp, obj = coordinate_descent_pass(best_a, alloc,
                                                     grid_points=14, top_k=8,
                                                     max_candidates=28)
            else:
                alloc = min(30.0, rem)
                a_tmp, obj = transfer_polish(best_a, alloc, grid_points=16,
                                             rng_seed=seed + phase * 7)
        elif p == 2:
            alloc = min(35.0, rem)
            a_tmp, obj = transfer_polish(best_a, alloc, grid_points=20,
                                         rng_seed=seed + phase * 11)
        elif p == 3:
            alloc = min(22.0, rem)
            a_tmp, obj = smooth_descent(best_a, alloc, lr=0.003, mom=0.92,
                                        beta_start=50.0, beta_end=3000.0,
                                        seed=seed + phase * 13)
        elif p == 4:
            if use_lp and rem > 120.0:
                alloc = min(55.0, rem)
                a_tmp, obj = lp_polish(best_a, alloc, seed=seed + phase * 17,
                                       max_solves=2)
            else:
                alloc = min(30.0, rem)
                a_tmp, obj = transfer_polish(best_a, alloc, grid_points=14,
                                             rng_seed=seed + phase * 19)
        elif p == 5:
            if use_coord:
                alloc = min(45.0, rem)
                a_tmp, obj = coordinate_descent_pass(best_a, alloc,
                                                     grid_points=12, top_k=6,
                                                     max_candidates=22)
            else:
                alloc = min(25.0, rem)
                a_tmp, obj = transfer_polish(best_a, alloc, grid_points=14,
                                             rng_seed=seed + phase * 23)
        elif p == 6:
            rng = np.random.default_rng(seed + phase * 29)
            scale = 0.003 * max(0.3, 1.0 - 0.02 * no_improve)
            a_noise = project_simplex(best_a + rng.standard_normal(n) * scale)
            alloc = min(28.0, rem)
            a_tmp, obj = smooth_descent(a_noise, alloc, lr=0.004, mom=0.90,
                                        beta_start=10.0, beta_end=1200.0,
                                        seed=seed + phase * 31)
        else:
            alloc = min(15.0, rem)
            a_tmp, obj = smooth_descent(best_a, alloc, lr=0.002, mom=0.95,
                                        beta_start=100.0, beta_end=12000.0,
                                        seed=seed + phase * 37)

        if np.isfinite(obj) and obj < best_obj - 1e-15:
            best_obj = float(obj)
            best_a = normalize_seq(a_tmp)
            no_improve = 0
            if verbose:
                print(f"  combined polish phase {phase}: obj={best_obj:.12f}")
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        phase += 1

    a = normalize_seq(best_a)
    conv = fast_convolve(a)
    obj = float(np.max(conv))
    return a, obj


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800):
    """
    Main search entry point. Uses the parent construction if available,
    plus tail/sinusoidal/random seeds, then repeatedly polishes and finalizes.
    """
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(120.0, float(budget_s) - 60.0)

    parent = None
    raw = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if raw is not None:
        try:
            par = np.asarray(raw, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if par.size > 0:
                par = np.where(np.isfinite(par), par, 0.0)
                par = np.maximum(par, 0.0)
                ps = float(par.sum())
                if ps > 1e-10:
                    parent = par / ps
        except Exception:
            parent = None

    if parent is None:
        parent = make_alpha_tail_seed(768, 0.5)

    best_a = normalize_seq(parent)
    try:
        best_score = float(evaluate_sequence(best_a.tolist()))
    except Exception:
        best_score = quick_score(best_a)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        best_score = quick_score(best_a)
    print(f"Initial: n={best_a.size}, score={best_score:.6f}")

    base_n = int(best_a.size)

    # Cheap global parameter scan for tail shapes.
    scan_ns = [base_n]
    if base_n != 1024:
        scan_ns.append(1024)

    quick_best_qs = float("inf")
    quick_param = (base_n, 0.5, 0.0)
    alphas = [0.35, 0.40, 0.45, 0.50, 0.55, 0.60, 0.65, 0.70, 0.80, 0.90, 1.00]
    tilts = [-0.10, 0.00, 0.10, 0.20]

    for nscan in scan_ns:
        for alpha in alphas:
            for tilt in tilts:
                a = make_tilted_tail_seed(nscan, alpha, tilt)
                qs = quick_score(a)
                if np.isfinite(qs):
                    if qs < quick_best_qs:
                        quick_best_qs = qs
                        quick_param = (int(nscan), float(alpha), float(tilt))
                    if qs < best_score - 1e-8:
                        best_a, best_score = try_candidate(
                            a, best_a, best_score,
                            f"scan_{nscan}_{alpha}_{tilt}"
                        )

    # First, intensively polish the current best (usually the parent).
    now = time.monotonic()
    rem = deadline - now
    if rem > 120.0:
        alloc = min(360.0, rem - 120.0)
        if alloc > 20.0:
            print("Polishing current best")
            a_tmp, obj = combined_polish(best_a, alloc, verbose=True, seed=int(seed))
            best_a, best_score = try_candidate(a_tmp, best_a, best_score, "polish_best")

    # Build a diverse seed list and polish the most promising ones.
    seed_infos = []
    nq, aq, tq = quick_param
    for delta in (-0.05, 0.0, 0.05):
        add_seed_info(seed_infos,
                      f"quick_{nq}_{aq + delta}_{tq}",
                      make_tilted_tail_seed(int(nq), float(aq + delta), float(tq)))

    for alpha in (0.45, 0.50, 0.55, 0.60):
        add_seed_info(seed_infos,
                      f"base_{base_n}_{alpha}",
                      make_alpha_tail_seed(base_n, alpha))

    n_large = int(max(1024, min(2048, base_n * 1.33)))
    if n_large != base_n:
        for alpha in (float(aq), 0.5):
            add_seed_info(seed_infos,
                          f"large_{n_large}_{alpha}",
                          make_alpha_tail_seed(n_large, alpha))

    add_seed_info(seed_infos, f"sine_{base_n}", make_sine_seed(base_n, 1, 0.8, 0.0))
    add_seed_info(seed_infos, f"rand_{base_n}",
                  make_random_antisy_seed(base_n, 8, 0.9, int(seed) + 1))
    add_seed_info(seed_infos, "comp_best", project_complement(best_a))

    if not seed_infos:
        add_seed_info(seed_infos, "fallback_tail", make_alpha_tail_seed(base_n, 0.5))

    seed_infos.sort(key=seed_score_key)
    seed_infos = seed_infos[:8]

    for idx, item in enumerate(seed_infos):
        label = item[0]
        a_seed = item[1]
        now = time.monotonic()
        rem = deadline - now
        if rem < 130.0:
            break
        if idx == 0:
            alloc = min(180.0, rem - 130.0)
        elif idx < 3:
            alloc = min(120.0, rem - 130.0)
        else:
            alloc = min(60.0, rem - 130.0)
        if alloc < 20.0:
            break
        print(f"Polish seed {label}: quick={item[2]:.6f}, alloc={alloc:.0f}")
        a_tmp, obj = combined_polish(a_seed, alloc, verbose=False,
                                     seed=int(seed) + 1000 + idx * 17)
        best_a, best_score = try_candidate(a_tmp, best_a, best_score, label)

    # Final intensive fixed-length polish on the best candidate.
    now = time.monotonic()
    rem = deadline - now
    if rem > 100.0 and best_a.size <= 1100:
        alloc = min(200.0, rem - 100.0)
        if alloc > 20.0:
            a_tmp, obj = coordinate_descent_pass(best_a, alloc,
                                                 grid_points=18,
                                                 top_k=10,
                                                 max_candidates=32)
            best_a, best_score = try_candidate(a_tmp, best_a, best_score, "final_cd")

    now = time.monotonic()
    rem = deadline - now
    if rem > 90.0:
        alloc = min(90.0, rem - 90.0)
        if alloc > 15.0:
            a_tmp, obj = transfer_polish(best_a, alloc,
                                         grid_points=24,
                                         rng_seed=int(seed) + 777)
            best_a, best_score = try_candidate(a_tmp, best_a, best_score, "final_transfer")

    now = time.monotonic()
    rem = deadline - now
    if rem > 70.0:
        alloc = min(60.0, rem - 70.0)
        if alloc > 10.0:
            a_tmp, obj = smooth_descent(best_a, alloc, lr=0.002, mom=0.95,
                                        beta_start=100.0, beta_end=30000.0,
                                        seed=int(seed) + 991)
            best_a, best_score = try_candidate(a_tmp, best_a, best_score, "final_smooth")

    # Optional final LP direction if we still have generous time and reasonable n.
    if SCIPY_OPT_OK and best_a.size <= 800:
        now = time.monotonic()
        rem = deadline - now
        if rem > 180.0:
            alloc = min(80.0, rem - 80.0)
            if alloc > 30.0:
                a_tmp, obj = lp_polish(best_a, alloc, seed=int(seed) + 2023,
                                       max_solves=3)
                best_a, best_score = try_candidate(a_tmp, best_a,
                                                   best_score, "final_lp")

    # Final verification and return.
    best_a = normalize_seq(best_a)
    try:
        final_score = float(evaluate_sequence(best_a.tolist()))
    except Exception:
        final_score = quick_score(best_a)
    if not np.isfinite(final_score):
        final_score = quick_score(best_a)
    print(f"FINAL SCORE: {final_score:.6f} n={best_a.size}")
    return [float(x) for x in best_a]
```
